# Field question bank — seed (stage 0, revised 2026-11-09)

> **Коротко українською.** 1329 унікальних запитань (18 варіантів злито). **Чесний склад джерел:** 1 127 рядків (85%) —
> це списки запитань, які DOU зібрав в інтерв'юерів (2020–2023), а не звіти кандидатів; 202 рядки (15%) — звіти кандидатів,
> публічні розбори, офіційні сторінки платформ і агрегатори. Через авторське право рядки з DOU тепер подано **короткими
> нейтральними переказами** тією ж мовою (посилання, дата й id збережено); дослівно лишено лише 37 коротких (≤ 12 слів)
> запитань від кандидатів з атрибуцією. Частота тепер рахує **лише власні джерела запитання**: very common 1,
> common 13, rare 1315 — тобто банк здебільшого одноджерельний; повторюваність концепту винесено в окрему
> колонку. Для кожного запитання є «плановий дім» — курс і розділ із реєстру/outline: **1283 з 1329 = 96.5%**;
> 46 запитань без дому — це прогалини для авторів outline (список унизу).

Compiled 2026-11-08, revised 2026-11-09 after `docs/fullstack/review-stage0.md` (M6, minors 9–11).

## Source mix (read this first)

| Kind | Rows | What it is |
|---|---|---|
| **Interviewer lists** (DOU JS 300+, Front-end 400+, Node.js 255, DevOps 300+) | 1,127 (85%) | questions DOU collected from people who run interviews in Ukraine, 2020-12 … 2023-12; topic lists, not reports of a specific interview; stored as paraphrases |
| **Candidate / first-hand reports** (Glassdoor via search snippets, HN, Blind, dev.to, DOU forum, LinkedIn, LeetCode Discuss) | 127 | what a candidate or interviewer says was actually asked |
| **Official platform / company pages** (Lemon.io, Arc, Amazon, DoorDash) | 16 | the company describing its own process |
| **Aggregated reports** (aicodinginterview.com, prepfully, PracHub, techinterview.org, 1point3acres …) | 51 | third parties compiling candidate reports — treat as "reported" |
| **Prep guides** | 8 | only where no first-hand evidence was found (Next.js App Router, AI engineering) |

So the bank is strong on **breadth of theory topics asked in Ukraine** and thin on **first-hand, company-specific reports**.

## Wording policy (copyright)

- `paraphrase` (1152 rows): a short neutral restatement in the original language; never more than a few
  consecutive words of the source except technical terms. The source link stays in every row.
- `quote` (37 rows): verbatim only when ≤ 12 words and candidate- or interviewer-reported, with attribution.
- `summary` (140 rows): our own one-line description of a reported task or process step (not the source text).

## Frequency — the rule (revised)

`frequency` counts **only the question's own distinct sources** (after merging variants): **very common** ≥ 3 sources,
**common** = 2, **rare** = 1; the number in brackets is the count. Result: very common 1, common
13, rare 1315. This is honest but weak: most questions were seen in one source, because the DOU lists
rarely repeat each other word for word. The separate column `concept sources` gives the number of distinct sources that
ask *anything* in the same concept cluster (≈70 regex clusters such as "event loop", "CORS", "transactions/ACID"). Use it
to prioritise topics, never to label a single question. Frequency will become meaningful once Reddit exports and more
candidate reports are added (see the plan below).

## Topic, skill and planned home

- Topic and skill were re-tagged row by row (Sonnet, 8 batches; Opus spot check of 22 random rows plus all NONE rows).
  New topics: **Architecture** (code-level design: OOP, SOLID, GoF/JS patterns, DDD in code — e.g. fq-0854 Module
  pattern → Architecture / `module-pattern`) and **Leadership**. Skill is a short kebab-case concept.
- `planned home` = canonical course id from `docs/fullstack/registry.md` + section slug from the outline files, chosen
  by concept owner rules (registry §4). It is a **plan**, not a link to a written lesson or exercise: lesson/exercise
  coverage stays 0% until stage 1 creates topic ids.

## Open gaps and plan

| Gap | Size now | Plan |
|---|---|---|
| **Reddit** (r/cscareerquestions, r/ExperiencedDevs, r/webdev, r/freelance) | 0 rows — HTTP 403 for every fetch method | owner supplies exports (Reddit data export or saved threads as HTML/JSON — we must not create accounts); researcher mines them with the same pipeline; target +150 first-hand rows |
| **Lead / Tech Lead** | 3 rows at lead level, 6 Leadership rows | mine engineering-manager / staff write-ups (Pragmatic Engineer, LeadDev, StaffEng stories), Amazon bar-raiser reports, and the owner's own lead interviews; target ≥ 60 rows mapped to `fs-lead/S1…S11` |
| **SQL coding tasks** (windows, gaps-and-islands, top-N, EXPLAIN) | ~10 task-style rows | first-hand SQL tasks are rare in public reports; source them from interviewer write-ups and LeetCode/StrataScratch *company tags* (link only, no statements), and from the owner; the §5.1 SQL set itself is authored, not mined |
| **Glassdoor full text** | snippets only (403) | owner exports or manual copy of selected review pages |
| **Djinni** | no interview reviews exist | none — keep Djinni for salary data |
| **Next.js App Router / RSC, AI engineering** | prep-guide evidence only | look for 2026 candidate reports on Blind / LeetCode Discuss / dev.to; replace prep rows |
| **DOU lists are 2020–2023** | 85% of rows | refresh with DOU forum threads and 2025–2026 company reviews |
| **Questions with no planned home** | 46 rows (list below) | outline authors decide: add a topic, or mark out of scope (Vue/Sass/deep Linux networking) |
